{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"carries on","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"carrying on","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"carried on","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"carried on","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"I’ll be gone for a few days, but I hope you will carry on in my absence.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To continue or proceed as before."],"tags":["idiomatic","intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"to carry on commerce in a market","type":"example"},{"text":"Carry on the good work.","type":"example"},{"text":"It is difficult to carry on a conversation with so many distractions.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To continue, maintain or pursue (an activity or enterprise)."],"tags":["idiomatic","transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"keep calm and carry on","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To persist, persevere."],"synonyms":[{"word":"keep on"},{"word":"hang in"},{"word":"hang on"},{"word":"keep on keeping on"}],"tags":["idiomatic","intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He’s always carrying on about his stupid aquarium.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To talk continuously or prolongedly (about something), often in an excessively excited way."],"synonyms":[{"word":"go on"}],"tags":["idiomatic","intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I really wish you wouldn't carry on like that in public!","type":"example"},{"text":"I could hear some protestors hollering, but I wasn't close enough to make out what was happening. What were they carrying on about?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To act or behave; especially, to misbehave so as to attract attention; to make a fuss; to behave ostentatiously."],"synonyms":[{"word":"see misbehave § Synonyms"}],"tags":["idiomatic","intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I thought he was my friend, but all the time he was carrying on with my wife!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To have an illicit sexual or flirtatious relationship."],"tags":["idiomatic","intransitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈkæɹi ˈɑn/","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"/ˈkaɾe ˈɔn/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/ˈkaɾi ˈɔn/","tags":["Scotland"]}],"upstream_line":467225,"upstream_offset":2192577579,"upstream_record_sha256":"ae6ee78ca346471217f76b09e6f6759d8e55cfc742104b7eeeeea7172dcef23d","word":"carry on"}

{"forms":[{"form":"pearls","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A shelly concretion, usually rounded, and having a brilliant luster, with varying tints, found in the mantle, or between the mantle and shell, of certain bivalve mollusks, especially in the pearl oysters and river mussels, and sometimes in certain univalves.
